# CHANGELOG.md

Newest first. What was done, decided, and got wrong.

## 2026-11-13 — run 36: the explanation of the cosmological idea's seventh paragraph enters the map whole, A 547 / B 575 (AA 3:371.15–26), five sentences on reason's causality and the ought; three claims, all on no row of either comparison: that reason has causality, or at least that we represent to ourselves a causality of that kind in it, is clear from the imperatives which we in everything practical assign as rules to the executing powers; the ought expresses a kind of necessity and connection with grounds that otherwise does not occur in the whole of nature; it is impossible that something in nature ought to be otherwise than it in fact is in all these temporal relations, and the ought, if one has merely the course of nature before one's eyes, has no meaning at all; the first tested by the row-3 criterion of 2026-11-10 and not set, the reason argued on the page

Thirty-sixth scheduled run, on `main`, on the configured model `claude-fable-5-1` (the same the last entry records; no
handover). Read all governing files first (`DESIGN.md` to its *Decisions* section; nothing on the site's design
changed). The run fired the day after run 35, as that entry asked. The container's checkout again began detached at
run 35's commit with the local refs behind; `git fetch origin main` and a fast-forward came first, the changelog read
after.

**Step 1.** No ask changed status. Asks 4 (deferred), 9 and 10 stay open; none blocks the plan.

**Step 2.** `INBOX.md` carries the owner notes of 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-19, each handled in the session that received
it; nothing new (last changed 2026-10-19 by the git history). No dispute, no attestation, no form filing (the workflow
made no commit).

**Step 3.** No open motion.

**Step 4, the nodes: 4 new (1 passage, 3 claims), 8 files revised in their bodies (the will page in six places, the
first page in three, the four earlier passages of the explanation and the sixth paragraph's passage, README's count).**
The entry of 2026-11-12 named as the next content item the explanation's seventh paragraph from 3:371.15, delimited on
korpora's rows and the scans before entry, its sentences tested by the row-3 criterion and by the row on freedom's
words, with the question whether an »Imperativ« or »Sollen« sentence belongs to the row on what is drawn from the will
decided on the page; the run did that and nothing more.

*Delimiting.* Korpora's rows put a blank row before 3:371.15 and the next before 3:371.27; the 1781 edition indents
»Daß diese Vernunft« and »Dieses Sollen nun«, both on A 547, read on the scan today, so the two agree, and the
paragraph is five sentences on one page, between the sixth paragraph's »macht.« and the eighth's first line, which runs
on to A 548 with the catchword »Grund«; signature Mm 2; the running head of the ninth section. The B page by the
concordance, unverified.

*The passage,* `p-krv-a547-b575-that-reason-has-causality-is-clear-from-the-imperatives-and-the-ought-has-no-meaning-for-the-mere-course-of-nature`
(818 characters, five sentences by full stop and question mark, none a question; the semicolon of the fourth and the
two of the fifth join halves), extracted from korpora's rows line by line and asserted equal to the stored original;
nothing set off on korpora; the 1781 edition letterspaces »Imperativen«, »was da ist,« and »seyn soll,« and sets
»Sollen« in a larger face, emphases the map's text does not carry, named. The collation, from a token-by-token diff of
the Akademie text against the transcription and from the scan: nine orthographic forms (»Caussalität«, »Practischen«,
»vorkomt«, »kan«, »seyn« twice, »ia«, »blos«, »leztere«), eight punctuation variants, one word form (»vor
Eigenschaften«, the »vor« for »für« of A 537 and A 546), and one variant the scan has and the transcription does not:
no comma after »vorstellen« before »ist aus«, where the transcription prints one, as run 35 found »empirische Fragen«
on A 545. Terms and pronouns on the passage: »Sollen« "the ought" and »soll« "ought to", against B 308's »entsprechen
sollen« "are to" and beside A 534's »habe geschehen sollen«; »Bedeutung« "meaning" as at A 541, "sense" at B 307–312;
»ja« "indeed" against A 537's "after all"; »die ... vorkommt« singular, so »Art«, »Nothwendigkeit« or »Verknüpfung«
and not »Gründen«, read as the kind; »dieser« in the third sentence feminine singular, read as nature with the three
other candidates named; »darin« nature; »diesen Zeitverhältnissen« the third sentence's what is, has been, will be;
»der letztere« the circle. the stem of »Imperativen« stands in one other passage, the Fabel's stage 3, »ein Imperativ« of the true world, in
no claim (the first draft said "no other passage", the review's first serious finding); »Wollen« and »Willkür« stand in
the eighth paragraph, in no passage, »Willkür« in the map at A 533–534; nothing in the paragraph names the will, freedom,
the thing in itself or appearance.

*The three claims, all `draft`, each with its quote, none linked to anything.*
`c-kant-that-reason-has-causality-or-at-least-that-we-represent-one-in-it-is-clear-from-the-imperatives` (the first
sentence, 3:371.15–17): the statement drops »nun« and the demonstrative of »diese Vernunft«, which points back to the
sixth paragraph's last sentence, »Wir nennen diese Vermögen Verstand und Vernunft«, in that passage and in no claim,
and keeps the alternative »wenigstens« frames, so that what is asserted is that it is clear from the imperatives that
reason has causality, or at least that we represent to ourselves a causality of that kind in it, the weaker limb
asserted at least. Tested by the row-3 criterion of 2026-11-10 (a cause that belongs to the world of sense whose
causality is named as other than merely empirical): the sentence names a cause, reason, and its causality, and names
the causality neither as merely empirical nor as other, and no cause of the world of sense; the map resolves
»diese Vernunft« to the sixth paragraph's faculty, and so read the sentence's cause is the faculty with respect to
which man is to himself a merely intelligible object, which no sentence in the map places in the world of sense, so
the first limb fails, the thirteenth sentence's »von allen empirisch bedingten Kräften unterschieden« being of the
power, not its causality; the sentence would pass only if its cause were man, one of the natural causes by the eighth
sentence, and reason his faculty, an identification across three sentences of two paragraphs that A 545's sentence in
the cell makes inside itself and A 544's explication inside itself; the map reads the criterion, since today and as
its own reading, as met by what a sentence names in its own words with identifications of those words only, dated on
the page, does not make the identification and sets the sentence on no row, said on the node, in the row note and
under *On the will as cause*, open to dispute, a reader who holds that the eighth sentence's man and this sentence's
reason make one cause may ask. The first draft had argued the refusal the other way round, that the map "does not
make" the resolution it had just made, and that the carried predicates would pass the criterion; the review (M6, J1)
showed that, carried in, they defeat the first limb, and the reason was rewritten on both nodes. The question the entry of 2026-11-12 left to the page, whether an imperative sentence
belongs to the row on what is drawn from the will, is answered no, under *On what is drawn from the will*: the
sentence draws reason's causality from the imperatives, not anything from the will, which it does not name; its
»Praktischen« and »Imperativen« are the Kant words in the map nearest the »Moral« of that row's cells, which the
paragraph does not name. Not in the row on freedom (no »Freiheit«, »frei«, »Willkür«), not on the first page.
`c-kant-the-ought-expresses-a-kind-of-necessity-and-connection-with-grounds-that-otherwise-does-not-occur-in-the-whole-of-nature`
(the second, 3:371.17–19): the sentence whole; says what the ought expresses and that this does not occur in nature
otherwise, not that there is such a necessity, the map's reading of »drückt ... aus«; no cause, no causality, no row.
`c-kant-impossible-that-something-in-nature-ought-to-be-otherwise-than-it-is-and-the-ought-has-no-meaning-for-the-mere-course-of-nature`
(the fourth, 3:371.21–23): »darin« rendered "in nature", resolved through the third sentence's »dieser«; »diesen
Zeitverhältnissen« kept and resolved on the node, not written into the statement (run 34's J1); two assertions joined
by the semicolon and »ja«, an intensifier, the »wenn« condition on the second only; no row.
*Refusals by proposition on all three,* with the target's words and mode: A 534's practical freedom presupposing that
something »habe geschehen sollen« and a causality in the power of choice, the one other Kant sentence in the map that
joins an ought to a causality, not one proposition with any of the three and not contradictory with the fourth
sentence on the map's reading, open to dispute; A 533's two, the B xxvi footnote's »praktischen«, A 545's assumption
(whether this reason is a faculty of that assumption no passage says), A 546's man, A 538–541's four, A 544's two,
A 537's, B xxviii's two, A 542–543's law of nature (the nearest to the course of nature), A 543–544's four, A 543's
conditional, A 539's »denn« clause on time and the Aesthetic's time claim (no identification of the temporal relations
with time as condition), A 236 / B 295 (nearest the unclaimed third sentence); Schopenhauer's § 55 (negative freedom,
sharing »Nothwendigkeit« and »Grund« and no proposition; every thing as appearance necessary, whose »nothwendig« says
what a thing is where Kant's »unmöglich« governs an ought; the person never free; Kant first showed the coexistence),
§ 19's key; Nietzsche's GD 3 (»dass Wille ein Vermögen ist«, »Der Wille bewegt nichts mehr«, »Es giebt gar keine
geistigen Ursachen!«, the nearest to a causality of reason, which deny of the will and of spiritual causes what the
first sentence asserts of reason, the identification of reason with either the map's to make and not made, and the
weaker limb denied by nothing, no attack, open to dispute), JGB 36, JGB 19's »weshalb« clause, JGB 21's conventional
fictions (a »soll«), its invented »Zwang«, »Gesetz«, »Grund« (who invented the sign-world, not a denial that the ought
expresses a necessity found nowhere else in nature), its »"An-sich"« and »Folgen-Müssen«, GD 7's two, GD 8's two. The
two unclaimed sentences, the third (what the understanding can cognize of nature) and the fifth (what we cannot ask of
nature), each with what it says and the nearest claim, named on the passage; a reader may ask for either.
*Pages.* The will page: the row-3 note gains a sentence, *On the will as cause* a paragraph, *On what is drawn from
the will* a sentence, the gaps list the seventh paragraph as held and 3:371.27 as where the unheld text begins, the
closing paragraph the date, `passages:` the new passage; the first page the same in its gaps list, closing paragraph
and `passages:`; the four earlier passages of the explanation and the sixth paragraph's passage name the seventh as
held and the eighth paragraph as where the unheld text begins, the sixth's »Daß diese Vernunft nun Causalität habe«
now in guillemets as text in a passage. README counts 349 nodes (3 thinkers, 5 works, 99 passages, 220 claims, 16
arguments, 4 readings, 2 comparisons), as `--check` does. The guillemet check over the new claim bodies, run before the
review, found five quotations wrong and one running head quoted from no passage, all corrected before the review:
GD 3's »dass Wille ein Vermögen ist« (written with »der«), § 55's negative-freedom clause (its word order), JGB 21's
sentence on inventing causes (»Wir sind es, die allein ...«, written as »Es sind wir allein, welche ...«), »Folgen-Müssen«
(written lower-case), and »geistige Ursache« in the singular (the passage has the plural).

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the four new nodes, the diff of the eight revised
files, korpora's AA III 371–372, wikisource's transcriptions of A 547–548, the scan of A 547 with two enlarged crops,
the token-diff files, the rendered build, `MODEL.md` and the distilled rules, with the brief to re-extract the original,
collate the page against the transcription and the scan, test every qualifier, mode, pronoun and relative, every refusal
by proposition against cited and uncited claims and the unclaimed sentences, the placement of all five sentences under
the row-3 criterion with the precedents (A 545's S3 set, A 537's two and A 544's Q1 not), the row on what is drawn, the
accretion across the map and the rendering. It confirmed the original character for character (818 characters, five
sentences, three semicolons), the token diff yielding exactly the listed variants, the scan's two indents, running head,
signature, catchword, the three letterspaced terms and the larger »Sollen«, no comma visible after »vorstellen«, all
five sentences' English, every qualifier and mode, every pronoun's candidates, every cited node's words and mode, every
»...« in the new bodies inside a passage, the eight revised files' sentences true of the files, README's count, and six
rendered pages with all 1,204 internal links resolving; and returned 26 findings, 3 serious, 11 moderate, 12 minor, and
four judgements; all 26 accepted and changed before commit, all four judgements taken.
- *Serious, 3.* "»Imperativen«, a word in no other passage" was false: the Fabel's stage 3 has »ein Imperativ« of the
  true world, »königsbergisch«, in the passage and in no claim, now named on the passage, in the first claim's Nietzsche
  sweep and in the will page's row-5 paragraph, with the offer of stage 3 as a claim; "the one other Kant sentence in
  the map with »praktisch«" was false, the word standing at A 533–534 three times, B xxviii and B xxix–xxx, in claims
  the same body names, now listed; the »sollen« gloss named two of seven Kant places and "the one other Kant sentence
  with an ought" held only as "rendered 'ought'", now said so with all seven places, and § 55's unclaimed clause »kann
  daher in keiner Beziehung anders seyn, als es ist«, the Schopenhauer words nearest in form to the fourth sentence,
  now named on the third claim with the »kann« against »soll« contrast the refusal rests on.
- *Moderate, 11.* »Natur« counted four times where it stands three, and »Vernunft« misplaced; "»Wollen« and »Willkür«
  first occur in the eighth paragraph" unsourced for the first paragraph and false for the map; the »ja« gloss citing an
  "after all" at A 537 where no »ja« stands ("after all" renders »doch« there); the »Bedeutung« gloss misstating B 308
  ("all meaning of the categories") and omitting six places, now listed by rendering; »eben so wohl« at A 543–544, not
  A 537, and »vor ein Grund« on A 545, not A 546; the placement reason's mechanism wrong (above); the in-sentence limit
  of the criterion formulated today and undated, now dated on the page; the row-5 sentence calling the paragraph's two
  words "the words in the map's Kant passages nearest to »Moral«" a false superlative (B xxviii has »Moral«); the
  fourth sentence's statement keeping "these temporal relations" where the first drops its demonstrative, the reason now
  written; the scan's missing comma read without naming the comma-wide gap; the faces not counted term by term.
- *Minor, 12, all changed:* the third sentence's English commas the 1781 edition's, unnamed; »wenigstens«, »Praktischen«
  and »in der That« glosses naming one earlier place where several exist; the catchword made the page-end fragment by
  an apposition; "which runs on to A 548" attached to a word; the 1781 forms in guillemets against the no-passage rule,
  now reconciled in one sentence on the passage; the first claim's Kant sweep missing A 537's sixth sentence, A 538's
  nothing-hinders, A 539's every-cause-has-a-character and A 540's intellectual causality; the second claim's Nietzsche
  sweep missing JGB 21's »"Causal-Verknüpfung"« and »"psychologischer Nothwendigkeit"«; A 532–533 and A 540–541 cited by
  one page; summaries of unheld text beyond the reference; "set against" as a paraphrase of A 534.
- *Judgements, four, all taken:* the first sentence not set in the row on the will as cause, A 545's S3 not unset, the
  reason rewritten (J1); not in the row on what is drawn from the will (J2); not in the row on freedom nor on the first
  page (J3); no link due to A 534, § 55, JGB 21 or GD, the one gap the Fabel's stage 3 in no claim (J4).
The pass cost about 245,000 tokens of the agent's own and took fourteen minutes. New kinds today: a word-absence claim
that greps the word and not the stem; a placement reason that contradicts the node's own pronoun resolution; a gloss
citing a rendering at a page where the word does not stand. All in `memory.md`.

**What else was wrong and why.**
- The guillemet grep before the review caught five misquotations and a running head quoted from no passage (above);
  the build's own check, which covers the comparison pages only, then refused the will page's »ein Imperativ«, quoted
  from a passage the page does not list, reworded in plain words.
- `memory.md` went over its cap twice today (8,173 and 8,232 words) and was brought to 7,999 by compressing the run-34
  and run-35 blocks, the Nietzsche claims list, the Schopenhauer shared-premise list, the comparisons paragraph and two
  argument descriptions; their details are on the nodes and in the entries of 2026-11-11 and 2026-11-12.
- The stop hook asked for a commit while the reviewer ran; refused, as every content run has, and a watch on the report
  file was armed instead.

## 2026-11-12 — run 35: the explanation of the cosmological idea's sixth paragraph enters the map whole, A 544–547 / B 572–575 (AA 3:369.36–371.14), thirteen sentences from the principle of causality to the faculties called understanding and reason; three claims: supposing it should otherwise also be merely invented, it does the understanding not the least damage if one assumes that among the natural causes there are also some that have a faculty which is only intelligible, yet so that the action in appearance of this cause is in accordance with all laws of empirical causality, set in the Kant cell of the will page's row on the will as cause by the criterion of 2026-11-10 with two identifications named as the map's; man is one of the appearances of the world of sense and in so far also one of the natural causes whose causality must stand under empirical laws; man cognizes himself also through mere apperception and is to himself in one part phenomenon, in another, with respect to certain faculties, a merely intelligible object, both on no row

Thirty-fifth scheduled run, on `main`, on the configured model `claude-fable-5-1` (the same the last entry records; no
handover). Read all governing files first (`DESIGN.md` to its *Decisions* section; nothing on the site's design
changed). The run fired the day after run 34, as that entry asked. The container's checkout again began detached at
run 34's commit with the local refs behind; `git fetch origin main` and a fast-forward came first, the changelog read
after.

**Step 1.** No ask changed status. Asks 4 (deferred), 9 and 10 stay open; none blocks the plan.

**Step 2.** `INBOX.md` carries the owner notes of 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-19, each handled in the session that received
it; nothing new. No dispute, no attestation, no form filing (the workflow made no commit).

**Step 3.** No open motion.

**Step 4, the nodes: 4 new (1 passage, 3 claims), 8 files revised in their bodies (the will page in five places, the
first page in three, the four earlier passages of the explanation, the § 55 passage, README's count).** The entry of
2026-11-11 named as the next content item the explanation's sixth paragraph from 3:369.36, delimited on korpora's rows
and the scans before entry; the run did that and nothing more.

*Delimiting.* Korpora's rows put a blank row before 3:369.36 and the next before 3:371.15; the 1781 edition indents
»Wir bedürfen« on A 544 (scan opened on 2026-11-10) and »Daß diese Vernunft« on A 547 (scan opened today) and nothing
between, so the two agree and the sentences on man from 3:370.32 on stand inside the sixth paragraph, not in a seventh
of their own, the question the entry of 2026-11-10 left open. The paragraph runs over four pages: A 544 ends inside
»Eräugnissen« (catchword »äugnissen«), A 545 ends at »das Denken im reinen Verstande« (signature Mm, catchword »und,«),
A 546 at »weil die Handlung desselben« (catchword »gar«), A 547 carries the paragraph's end, »macht.«, and the seventh's
indent (signature Mm 2, catchword »Grund«); the scans of A 545, A 546 and A 547 were opened and read, the B pages by the
concordance, unverified.

*The passage,* `p-krv-a544-547-b572-575-the-principle-of-causality-natural-causes-with-a-faculty-that-is-only-intelligible-and-man-as-phenomenon-and-intelligible-object`
(3,675 characters, thirteen sentences by full stop and question mark, none a question; the colon of the fifth and the
semicolons of the sixth and thirteenth join halves), extracted from korpora's rows line by line and asserted equal to
the stored original; korpora's one italic tag, »causa phaenomenon« at 3:370.13, normalized to roman and named; the 1781
edition sets it and »phaenomenon« in antiqua and letterspaces »Handlung in der Erscheinung« on A 545, emphasis the map's
text does not carry. The collation, from a token-by-token diff of the Akademie text against the four transcriptions:
thirty-odd orthographic forms with their counts, thirteen punctuation variants, and three word forms, »physische
Erklärungen«, »vor ein Grund« (the »vor« for »für« A 537 also has) and, from the scan and not the transcription,
»empirische Fragen« (the review's finding). Terms and the pronouns on the passage: »ihrer« in »aller ihrer Handlungen«,
feminine or plural where »Subject« is neuter, read as the subject's by sense with nature named as the form's candidate;
»derselben« in »den bloß empirischen Charakter derselben« the cause's, the effects the other; »desselben« in »die
Handlung desselben« singular, so not the faculties, read as the intelligible object with man the other candidate;
»ihm« in the third sentence the understanding, with »Satzes der Causalität« named as the other masculine singular after
the review. »Apperception« and »Naturregel« occur in no other passage; »intelligibel« stands five times in the paragraph
and never of a cause, so A 541's count of three places stands. Ten sentences are in no claim, each named on the passage
with what it says and its test by the row-3 criterion.

*The three claims, all `draft`, each with its quote.*
`c-kant-assuming-natural-causes-with-a-faculty-that-is-only-intelligible-does-the-understanding-not-the-least-damage`
(the third sentence, 3:370.05–12, whole on A 545): the statement drops »Nun«, moves the »gesetzt« clause to the front so
that it reads alone, and asserts only what the sentence asserts, that the assumption does the understanding not the
least damage, the assumption itself standing under »wenn man annimmt« and its invented-ness conceded under »gesetzt«;
the English "it" does not resolve, said on the node after the review. Set in the Kant cell of the will page's row on
the will as cause by the criterion of 2026-11-10, with two identifications named as the map's on the node, in the row
note and under *On the will as cause*: that the natural causes the sentence names belong to the world of sense, which the
paragraph's eighth sentence says of man as one of them and A 544's explication of its cause, and no sentence says of
natural causes as such; and that a faculty »welches nur intelligibel ist«, whose determination to action rests on mere
grounds of the understanding, is what the criterion calls a causality named as other than merely empirical, as A 538's
second sentence, the first of the four in the cell, rests the two sides of a causality on a faculty; and, after the
review, that what the two A 537 claims lack, a sentence calling their cause one of the world of sense, this sentence has
from the eighth sentence of its own paragraph, the difference the setting rests on. Both open to dispute, and a reader
who holds that the sentence names no causality but the empirical one of its »so doch« clause may ask for it to be set on
no row. No link: the fourth sentence grounds it by »Denn« and is in no claim; A 538–539's double-side sentence, the
nearest in predicate and the nearest pair in the map to a shared premise inside one section of Kant, is refused with the
reason, the predicates and the subjects differing, open to dispute (the review's judgement J6, taken).
`c-kant-man-one-of-the-appearances-of-the-world-of-sense-and-one-of-the-natural-causes-whose-causality-must-stand-under-empirical-laws`
(the eighth, 3:370.33–35, whole on A 546): the sentence whole; names man's causality only as one that must stand under
empirical laws, so it stands to the criterion as the fifth paragraph's first question does and is on no row; the ninth
draws from it by »demnach« and is in no claim.
`c-kant-man-cognizes-himself-through-mere-apperception-and-is-to-himself-in-one-part-phenomenon-in-another-part-a-merely-intelligible-object`
(the twelfth, 3:371.02–09, A 546–547): »Allein« dropped and named; on no row of the will page (names faculties, an action
and an object, no causality; neither the will nor »unmittelbar«, so not in the row on immediacy, where B xxviii's
sentence on cognizing the soul is the nearest row-mate, named after the review, and a sentence added to that row's note)
and on no row of the first page: it divides man as phenomenon from man as intelligible object, and whether that
intelligible object is the object B 312's sentence in the division row says is taken as noumenon when thought as in
itself merely intelligible is an identification the map does not make, named on the node and the page after the review.
Refusals by proposition on all three, with the target's words and mode, among them A 538's definition and second
sentence, A 539–541's four, A 544's three, A 537's two, A 542–544's six, A 533's two, B xxvii, B xxviii, B 312; § 55,
§ 23, § 24, § 21, § 18, the Anhang; JGB 16 (both nodes), 17, 21, 36, 54, GD Irrthümer 3 and 8. The ten unclaimed
sentences were tested by the criterion: after the review, the sixth passes it (a cause in appearance whose intelligible
character is the transcendental cause of its empirical one, a character being by A 539 the law of a cause's causality,
as the cell's A 539 sentence is set) and is left unclaimed by this run's choice, and the fourth does not (the subject's

»in der Erscheinung« "in appearance" as at A 543, where A 538–539 has "in the appearance", named; »dennoch«
"nevertheless", »doch« "still"; »in Ansehung« "with respect to" as at A 537; »Glied« "member" as A 539's
»Glieder«; »Naturkette« "chain of nature", a word in no other passage; »Sinnenwelt« "world of sense"; »übrigens«
"otherwise", »gänzlich« "wholly", »mit zu ... gezählt« "counted among ... as well". Pronouns resolved on the
passage: »ihrer Ursache« twice, »ihren Zusammenhang«, Kant's own »(nämlich Ursache)« kept, and »die also in so
fern nicht Erscheinung, sondern ... intelligibel ist« bound to »Ursache«, the noun it follows and the one of which
a member of the chain of nature counted among the world of sense can be said, »Handlung« the other reading, named;
»diesem Vermögen« the faculty of such an original action, as the map reads the demonstrative.

*The three claims, all `draft`, each with its quote, one per sentence:*
`c-kant-is-it-necessary-that-the-causality-of-a-cause-that-is-itself-appearance-must-be-merely-empirical` and
`c-kant-is-it-not-rather-possible-that-empirical-causality-itself-be-an-effect-of-a-non-empirical-but-intelligible-causality`,
the two questions, kept as questions in the statement form the map gave JGB 34's close on 2026-10-21, "Kant asks
whether ...", the one earlier question-claim; the paragraph-opening »aber« and the »Und« joining the second to the
first dropped from the statements and named; what each asserts is nothing, and the bodies refuse to write the
expected answer into the statement; and
`c-kant-an-intelligible-causality-is-an-original-action-of-a-cause-that-is-in-so-far-not-appearance-but-intelligible`,
the »D. i.« sentence, whose statement words it as Kant's explication of his term, "as Kant explains the term in
asking whether ...", on the pattern of A 533's "as Kant understands the term", so that it claims what the sentence
says, what the term means, and not that there is such a causality; its genitive »einer ... Handlung einer Ursache«
read as parallel to »einer ... Causalität« under »Wirkung«, its »also« and the concessive »muß« left inside the
sentence. No link of any kind on the three. Refused and named, by proposition, with the target's words: A 538's
two sides and its definition of »intelligibel« (the third sentence's »nicht Erscheinung, sondern ... intelligibel«
the definition's two words in its order, the application the map's reading); A 538–539's permission to attribute a
causality that is not appearance to the transcendental object; A 539's intellectual causality not in the series,
its intelligible character and its »Glieder einer einzigen Reihe«; A 540's actions explicable by laws of nature;
A 541's active being beginning its effects of itself; A 537's effect regardable as free; A 544's own »Eine
ursprüngliche Handlung ... nicht zu erwarten«, the one other place where Kant writes »ursprünglich« of an action,
the two read as one original action by the map only; A 533's spontaneity and its faculty of beginning a state of
itself; A 542–543's law, which the second question's concessive clause concedes in other words; A 543's question
of 2026-11-09, which the map reads the second question as asking again with »aus Freiheit« become »einer
nichtempirischen, sondern intelligibelen Causalität«, open to dispute; Schopenhauer's § 55 on the two characters
and the intelligible character as an act of will, and its »durchweg nothwendig«; Nietzsche's JGB 21 on the
»"An-sich"« and on cause and effect, and GD's »Der Wille bewegt nichts mehr«. A question asserts nothing a premise
could share or an attack could meet; the explication says what a term means and not that there is such a cause.

*Placement, after the review.* The second question and the explication are set in the Kant cell of the will page's
row on the will as cause, after the four sentences of A 538–541 set there since 2026-10-19, for the same words,
»Wirkung«, »Ursache« and »Handlung«, and for a criterion formulated today as the map's reading of what the four
share: a cause that belongs to the world of sense whose causality is named as other than merely empirical,
intelligible; the first question, which names only the empirical causality, is on no row, as A 542–544's six
sentences are; the row's note says that what the cell gains is a question and an explication, with A 543's question
in the row on freedom since 2026-11-09 and JGB 34's on the first page as precedent. Tested by the same criterion
and not set, each with its reason in the new paragraph under *On the will as cause*: A 542–544's six sentences on
the law of nature and on what the causes in appearance alone cannot do, which name no causality other than the
empirical and stay on no row; A 538–539's permission on the transcendental object, which no sentence calls a cause
in the world of sense; A 539's three sentences on the characters and the series; A 538's definition, which names

(none names the thing in itself or the division; the third's "not appearance but intelligible" names the third
term, as that page says of A 538's definition). The will page's row note, the *On the will as cause* paragraph,
the *On freedom* sentence on the three, the two "nearest unset" re-tests, the gaps list, `passages:` and the
closing paragraph are dated; the first page's gaps list (the explanation now in a twelfth passage) and closing

3:369.36 as where the unheld text begins; the § 55 passage's list of pages held; the four cell claims, A 538's

2 comparisons), as `--check` does.

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the four new nodes, the diff of the sixteen
revised files, the korpora pages AA III 369–370, the wikisource transcriptions of A 544 and A 545, the scan of A 544,
the older nodes the new ones cite, the rendered build, `MODEL.md` and the distilled rules, with the brief to re-extract
the original, collate the 1781 page, test every qualifier, mode and pronoun, every refused link by proposition against
cited and uncited claims and the unclaimed sentences, the placement both ways, the accretion across the map and the
rendering. It fetched in addition korpora's AA III 368. It confirmed the original character for character (856
characters, no face markup), every listed 1781 variant, the indent, the one-sentence setting, »einer Wirkung«, the
catchword, nothing letterspaced, the heading's lines and the block count, the build, the links on the six built pages,
the cell's order, every »...« inside a listed passage, the YAML, »Naturkette« in no other passage and A 543–544's as
the one other »ursprünglich« of an action; and returned 31 findings, 4 serious, 12 moderate, 15 minor, and seven
judgements; all 31 accepted and changed before commit, four judgements taken, one deferred to the plan.
- *Serious, 4.* »Caussalität« stands four times in the 1781 paragraph, not three. The cell's criterion, "the causality
  of a cause in the world of sense being other than merely empirical", was written today and dated 2026-10-19 on every
  node and the page; it is now dated today and called the map's reading, worded as a cause that belongs to the world of
  sense whose causality is named as other than merely empirical. The proposition sweep had missed the map's nearest
  Kant sentences, A 536–537's unclaimed fifth and sixth, on the intelligible cause outside the series with its effects
  in it; named now on both cell claims and the page, and the superlatives re-weighed. The row-mate sweep named no
  Schopenhauer or Nietzsche row-mate; every row-mate of the three thinkers is now named on the two cell claims, JGB 36's
  will-causality as the nearest.
- *Moderate, 12.* "Said on the nodes" was false for the A 543 question node, which now carries the sentence; the
  "nearest unset" re-test stood on the page only and now stands on the five carrier nodes, the § 55 node's stale
  "A 537 the nearest" corrected; the binding of »die also in so fern« to »Ursache« rested on an action's not being a
  member of the chain of nature, which A 543–544's »Jede Handlung ... ist selbst Begebenheit« contradicts, and now rests
  on »diesem Vermögen nach«; the 1781 »einer Wirkung« lets the »d. i.« take up the effect, said now; "six places" named
  eight, now uncounted; **the first question passed a criterion that excluded A 543–544's original-action sentence, so,
  taking the reviewer's judgement, it is set on no row and only the second question and the explication stay in the
  cell**; the 1781 one-sentence setting was used as a cell reason and is now a fact only; A 541's »intelligibelen oder
  sensibelen Ursache«, A 540–541's intelligible character and Schopenhauer's § 24 on causality are named as refusals;
  A 538's unclaimed »bei einer und derselben Wirkung« sentence is named; "counted ... among the world of sense" became
  "reckoned ... to the world of sense" in translation, quotes, statement and title; the law-of-nature, effect-regardable
  and intellectual-causality nodes carry their dated sentence; the section's opening sentence names A 544.
- *Minor, 15, all changed:* »Sinnenwelt« "as throughout the map" (Prol § 57 has "sensible world"); the divergent earlier
  renderings of »in Ansehung«, »in der Erscheinung«, »allerdings« and »gänzlich« named; »diese empirische Causalität«
  resolved to the causality, not the connection; "every cause" imported the second question's »jeder« into the first,
  in nine places; the reason-creates sentence misparsed and cited A 544 for A 543–544; "say" for conceding clauses;
  "a faculty" for "this faculty"; the eleventh and twelfth passages without page references; the passage title without
  the explication; "d. i." and "would be" on the A 543–544 passage; § 23's »durchaus dem Satz vom Grunde unterworfen«
  weighed on the first question.
- *Judgements, seven:* the first question into the prose, taken; the "By ... Kant means, in asking whether ..." frame for
  the explication's statement, taken; A 538's faculty named as the other referent of »diesem Vermögen«, taken; the
  nothing-hinders sentence the nearer in predicate, taken; the »also« step spelt out, already there; the idle
  superlative dropped, taken; making A 536–537's fifth and sixth sentences claims before holding a term's explication,
  deferred to tomorrow with the reason (the map now holds the explication of »intelligibele Causalität« while the only
  Kant sentences asserting the term's bearer outright sit unclaimed since 2026-10-27).
The pass cost about 214,000 tokens of the agent's own and took fourteen minutes. New kinds today: a criterion dated to a
day it was not written; the row-mate sweep stopping at the thinker whose text was added; a count of a repeated 1781
form. All in `memory.md`.

**What else was wrong and why.**
- The first page's gaps list quoted the new passage's »nicht Erscheinung, sondern diesem Vermögen nach
  intelligibel« without listing the passage; the build's P1 check caught it, and the clause now refers without

- The three new claims were first all set in the cell, with the first question admitted on the 1781 typography; the

- **The first commit of this run (`d85c673`) pushed the nodes without this changelog entry and with `memory.md` at

## 2026-11-09 — run 32: the explanation of the cosmological idea's third paragraph enters the map, A 543 / B 571: the scruple that the matter looks like a chain of causes permitting no absolute totality has already been removed in the general assessment of the antinomy; if we want to yield to the deception of transcendental realism, neither nature nor freedom remains; and the subsection's question, whether the same event that is a mere effect of nature can nonetheless be regarded as an effect from freedom, or whether a direct contradiction is found between the two kinds of causality; three claims, two set in the Kant cell of the will page's row on freedom for the word »Freiheit«, one on no row

Thirty-second scheduled run, on `main`, on the configured model `claude-fable-5-1` (the same the last entry
records; no handover). Read all governing files first (`DESIGN.md` by its headings, since nothing on the site's
design changed). The run fired the day after run 31, as that entry asked. **The container's checkout began
detached at run 31's commit while its local `main` and `origin/main` refs stood a commit behind, at run 30's;
the first reading of the changelog therefore showed run 30 as the latest entry, and only `git fetch origin main`
brought run 31's commit, after which `main` was fast-forwarded before anything was written.** The rule is in
`memory.md` under *Tooling*: fetch first, then read.

**Step 1.** No ask changed status. Asks 4 (deferred), 9 and 10 stay open; none blocks the plan.

**Step 2.** `INBOX.md` carries the owner notes of 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-19, each handled in the session
that received it; nothing new. No dispute, no attestation, no form filing (the workflow made no commit).

**Step 3.** No open motion.

**Step 4, the nodes: 4 new (1 passage, 3 claims), 13 revised in their bodies (the will page in eight places,
the first page in four, the run-31 passage and claim, the A 543–544 passage, seven Kant claims, one Schopenhauer
claim, one Schopenhauer passage), README's count.** The entry of 2026-11-08 named as the next content item the
Erläuterung's third paragraph, AA 3:369.01–12, three sentences, from korpora with the A page on the scan opened
that day; the run did that and nothing more.

*The passage,* `p-krv-a543-b571-the-question-whether-the-same-event-can-be-regarded-as-an-effect-of-nature-and-as-an-effect-from-freedom`
(AA 3:369.01–12, three sentences by the map's count, the first's semicolon joining its »denn« clause, the third's
colon joining »Hier ist nur die Frage« to its indirect question), from korpora's Akademie text extracted line by line
and asserted equal to the stored original; the A page read on wikisource's transcription of A 543 and on the scan
itself, which shows the paragraph whole on the page, indented, between the tail »licher« of »möglicher« and the
indent of »Unter den Ursachen«, so the span is A 543 / B 571, the B page by the concordance's offset of 28, said
unverified. The 1781 text differs in orthography (»hiebey«, »anerkent«, »sey«, »blosse«, »Caussalität«, »einer
Seits« and »anderer Seits« as two words), in one word form (»zween Arten«) and in one punctuation mark (a colon after
»nachgeben wollen«); wikisource marks »absolute Totalität« bold and the scan shows the two words letterspaced,
korpora nothing. Terms: »Bedenklichkeit« "scruple"; »gehoben worden« "has been removed", the verb »heben«, not the
»aufheben« the map renders "annul"; »Beurtheilung« "assessment"; »aufs Unbedingte ausgeht« "goes out to the
unconditioned"; »Täuschung« "deception", as the map has it in JGB 36 and GD Vernunft 6; »lauter« "nothing but";
»Naturwirkung« "effect of nature"; »Wirkung aus Freiheit« "effect from freedom", as A 532's »aus Freiheit«; »Arten
von Causalität« "kinds of causality", the English the map also has for A 532's »zweierlei Causalität«, named;
»gerader Widerspruch« "direct contradiction"; the two »gar« and the two »doch« kept. Five pronouns resolved on the
passage: »sie« in »denn sie ist« the scruple; »sie« in »wenn sie ... ausgeht« reason, the noun nearest, with the
antinomy, the assessment, the scruple, the chain and the totality the other feminine singulars weighed, and "reason"
supplied in the English after the review; »ihren« in »zu ihren Bedingungen« the causes', the chain's the other
reading, named after the review; »eben dieselbe« the event, the singular of »Begebenheiten«, "event" supplied in
the translation, the question, the series and the necessity the other feminine singulars before it and none an
effect; »diesen zwei Arten« the two the sentence names. »hiebei« read as the law of the paragraph before, the map's
reading, said on the claim; the opening »aber« dropped from translation and statement and named.

*The three claims, all `draft`, each with its quote, one per sentence:*
`c-kant-the-scruple-about-a-chain-of-causes-permitting-no-absolute-totality-does-not-hold-us-up-having-been-removed-in-the-general-assessment-of-the-antinomy`
(the concessive »Ob es aber gleich ... aussieht« conceding how the matter looks and asserting nothing of how it is;
the »denn« ground kept inside, since the general assessment of the antinomy of reason, what the map reads as a text,
is in no passage; the scruple's words those of A 533's »keine absolute Totalität der Bedingungen im
Causalverhältnisse«, the two totalities one as the map's reading, no connective, no link);
`c-kant-if-we-yield-to-the-deception-of-transcendental-realism-neither-nature-nor-freedom-remains` (a conditional
whose antecedent Kant does not assert, »wollen« kept; »transscendentalen Realismus« in no other passage of the map
and undefined by the sentence); and
`c-kant-the-only-question-is-whether-the-same-event-that-is-a-mere-effect-of-nature-can-be-regarded-as-an-effect-from-freedom`
(the sentence asserts only what the question is, in the main clause »Hier ist nur die Frage«, and neither answer,

any kind on the three. Refused and named, each with its reason: for the second, A 536's »sind Erscheinungen Dinge an
sich selbst, so ist Freiheit nicht zu retten« as nearest in form, whether its case is transcendental realism unsaid
in any passage, and A 536's next sentence keeping nature as the complete cause where this sentence leaves neither
nature nor freedom; A 534's »so würde die Aufhebung der transscendentalen Freiheit zugleich alle praktische Freiheit
vertilgen«, the other conditional of the same shape in the cell, after the review; B xxvii's two sentences; A 541's
»Freiheit und Natur ... zugleich« under »würde«; the Kant words for a deception in the map, A 536's »betrügliche
Voraussetzung« and A 235's »Scheins« and »täuscht«, after the review; § 55's »Jedes Ding ist als Erscheinung ...
nothwendig: dasselbe ist an sich Wille«; GD Vernunft 6's »moralisch-optische Täuschung«, in the passage and in no
claim; and the Fabel's stage 6, »mit der wahren Welt haben wir auch die scheinbare abgeschafft«, nearest in form
among Nietzsche's sentences, after the review. For the third, seven Kant places saying in their own modes what the
question asks, each joined by no connective: A 537's effect »kann ... als frei und doch zugleich ... angesehen
werden«, in the same verb; A 541's »ohne allen Widerstreit«, »Widerstreit« not »Widerspruch«; B xxvii–xxviii's
»ohne daß hiebei ein Widerspruch vorgeht« and B xxvii's »offenbaren Widerspruch«, of the will, which the question
does not name; and, after the review, B xxviii–xxix's freedom that »dem Naturmechanism eben derselben Handlung (in
anderer Beziehung genommen) gar kein Hinderniß in den Weg lege«, the nearest by proposition, in another row of the
same page; A 538's causality »auf zwei Seiten« under »kann man« with the unclaimed »bei einer und derselben Wirkung
zusammen stattfinden«; and A 538–539's double side that »widerspricht keinem von den Begriffen«; the two kinds of
causality A 532's pair as the map reads it; Schopenhauer's § 55, where Kant was the first to show »das Zusammenbestehn
dieser Nothwendigkeit mit der Freiheit des Willens an sich«, asserting what the question asks, and its unclaimed
»Vereinigung der Freiheit mit der Nothwendigkeit« the nearest Schopenhauer sentence in matter; Nietzsche's causa sui
as »Selbst-Widerspruch«, the unfree will, the feeling of unfreedom and »"intelligible Freiheit"«. For the first,
»Antinomie«, »Regressus«, »Unbedingte« and »Kette von Ursachen« in no other passage, »Kette« only in JGB 19's chain
of inferences and »Totalität« at A 533 and in section III's heading, GD 8's »causa prima« the end of a regress in
other words, after the review.

*Placement.* The second and third sentences name »Freiheit« and are set in the Kant cell of the will page's row on
freedom, for that word, as every sentence of the resolution in the map that names it is, after the eleven A 532–537
claims; what the third sets there is a question, said in the row's note, with the thing-in-itself page's Nietzsche
cell on the division, which holds two questions kept as questions, as precedent. The first names neither the will
nor freedom nor the thing in itself and is set on no row. The second is not set in the first page's row on the

transcendental realism leaves and not what transcendental realism is, so whether its case is A 536's, appearances

would be the map's identification; the first page names it in its gaps list as among the nearest unset, with A 534's
conditional and B xxviii–xxix's clause on what the critique's restriction made possible, both on the will page and

asserting nothing. README counts 335 nodes (3 thinkers, 5 works, 96 passages, 209 claims, 16 arguments, 4 readings,
2 comparisons), as `--check` does.

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the four new nodes, the diff of the fourteen
revised files, the korpora pages AA III 368–370, the wikisource transcriptions of A 543 and A 544, the scan of

confirmed the original character for character (820 characters, three sentences, no face markup on korpora), the

»Regressus« and »Unbedingte« in no other passage, the rendered pages and the build; and returned 22 findings,
1 serious, 9 moderate, 12 minor, and six judgements; all 22 accepted and changed before commit, one judgement taken.
- *Serious, 1.* The sweep by proposition for the question counted "four places" and missed three Kant sentences
  in the map: B xxviii–xxix's »eben derselben Handlung (in anderer Beziehung genommen)«, the nearest by
  proposition, sitting in the will page's row on what is drawn; A 538's two sides with the unclaimed »einer und

much as the first; it preferred no cell. Taken: none of the four is set on a row; the row's note and a new
paragraph under *On freedom* name them as the map's reading of what the paragraph denies of every cause in
appearance; the first page is untouched in its rows, no sentence saying what divides appearances from things in
themselves. The will page's gaps list and closing paragraph are dated; its old sentence naming A 537 the nearest
unset premise to § 55 now says until today, A 543's second sentence, asserted outright of every action as
appearance, being nearer; the A 537 claim, the § 55 claim, the two § 55 nodes citing Kant's A 532–558 by range,
the first page's sentence on the resolution's passages (nine now, one beyond A 541) and four Kant claims (the
definition, the spontaneity, A 539's law, A 541's effects) carry a dated sentence. README counts 329 nodes
(3 thinkers, 5 works, 94 passages, 205 claims, 16 arguments, 4 readings, 2 comparisons), as `--check` does.

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the five new nodes, the diff of the six
revised files, the korpora pages AA III 368–370, the wikisource transcriptions of A 541–544, the scan of A 543,
the thirteen older nodes the new ones cite, the rendered build, `MODEL.md` and the distilled rules, with the
brief to re-extract the original, collate the 1781 pages, test every qualifier, mode and pronoun, the »Also«
link, every refused link by proposition, the placement, the "first place" claim, the gaps list, the B pages,
the accretion across the map and the rendering. It confirmed the original character for character, the line
ranges and the sentence count, the collation note complete and exact, the catchword and the page end on the
scan, the concordance offset, every quotation inside a stored passage, every qualifier and mode, the link and
its converse, A 536's sentence in no claim, the build, the README count and the rendering; and returned
20 findings, 3 serious, 8 moderate, 9 minor, and five judgements; all 20 accepted and changed before commit,
and the reviewer's preference on the one judgement that mattered, the placement, taken.
- *Serious, 3.* The row-mate sweep named A 533's definition, A 539 and A 541 and missed three claims already
  in the freedom cell and one unclaimed sentence of their passage: A 534's »eine Reihe von Begebenheiten ganz von
  selbst anzufangen«, nearer in words to the first sentence than the definition the node rested on; A 533's
  »weil« clause stating the law that the causality of a cause which itself happened must in turn have a cause,
  the third sentence's proposition, which the first claim had attributed to the spontaneity node instead of the
  node that states it; A 534's »jede Begebenheit ... bestimmt« under »wäre«; and A 532–533's »selbst wiederum eine
  Ursache«. All named now on the claims whose propositions they share. The will page's new sentence, "the first
  place among the Kant passages the map holds where the text denies, outright and in Kant's own voice, of every
  cause in appearance what the cosmological definition names", was false of A 532–533, which denies it in other
  words; restricted to the definition's words and the earlier sentence named. The first page's gaps list still said
  the resolution was held in eight passages and that beyond A 541 was in no passage.
- *Moderate, 8.* The two § 55 nodes citing A 532–558 by range were stale (the rule of run 21, missed); two
  "nearest unset premise" claims stood for one § 55 sentence, and the § 55 claim's "nearest Kant statements"
  was stale; "fourth paragraph" rested on the 1781 indents where korpora's rows make it the third block, unsaid;
  the paragraph after the span was called "two questions" and has three sentences by the map's count, the second
  question misdescribed; "in appearance" diverged from three earlier renderings unnamed; "the three say the same
  of the causes in appearance" failed for the spontaneity sentence; Nietzsche's »Causal-Verknüpfung« in the very
  passage cited, and three more JGB 21 claims and two Schopenhauer claims nearer than § 23's, were unnamed; the
  word criterion that set the cells was not applied to A 541's sentence already on the page (moot once no cell is
  set).
- *Minor, 9, all changed:* »frei« missing from the row's criterion; »Begebenheit« "as at A 533" for four places;
  the definition's »anzufangen« called »anfangen«; »welche« unresolved; A 539's law in a »daß« clause called

- *Judgements, five:* no cell, taken; "likewise" for »eben so wohl«, not taken, "just as much" keeping the
  degree of »eben so wohl«, recorded; "temporal sequence"/"temporal series", "event or occurrence" and the

The pass cost about 178,000 tokens of the agent's own and took twelve minutes. New kinds today: a row-mate sweep
that named the definition and not the cell's other claims; a "first place" claim tested against no earlier
passage; a paragraph count from one source where the two differ; a cell by a word one step removed. All in
`memory.md`.

**What else was wrong and why.**

- The revision script's last anchor was a no-op check that matched twice and aborted after the five real edits

  compressions below brought it to 7,993; each count asserted before the next edit, as the rule of 2026-11-02

## 2026-11-05 — run 29: the Transcendental Aesthetic's section on time enters the map, its first spans: § 4's second and third points, time a necessary representation given a priori; § 6's conclusion (a), time neither something subsisting for itself nor an objective determination attaching to things, formalized with its own grounds as the map's eighth Kant argument; and the paragraph after conclusion (c), time nothing when the objects are taken as they may be in themselves, a subjective condition of our intuition and in itself, apart from the subject, nothing; two premises on time entered beside the premises on space

Twenty-ninth scheduled run, on `main`, on the configured model `claude-fable-5-1` (the same the
last entry records; no handover). Read all governing files first. The run fired the day after
run 28; `origin/main` stood at run 28's commit when it began, one ahead of the container's
detached checkout, which was fast-forwarded before anything was written; nothing arrived in
between.

**Step 1.** No ask changed status. Asks 4 (deferred), 9 and 10 stay open; none blocks the plan.

**Step 2.** `INBOX.md` carries the owner notes of 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-19, each handled in the
session that received it; nothing new. No dispute, no attestation, no form filing (the workflow made
no commit).

**Step 3.** No open motion.

**Step 4, the nodes: 11 new (3 passages, 7 claims, 1 argument), 14 revised in their bodies or links, the first page touched in two rows, four shared entries, its passage list and six places of its prose, the will page in one sentence.**
The entry of 2026-11-04 named as the next content item "the Anhang's theorems on time from
zeno.org collated against Grisebach". The phrase named no span: the Anhang's paragraph on the
Transcendental Aesthetic (ZA II 536–537, in the map since 2026-10-14) endorses the Aesthetic's
»Lehrsätze« as a whole and names none, and a grep of the whole Anhang on zeno.org, fetched this
morning, finds no separate treatment of time in it; the first page's gaps list, which says of the
Anhang "so neither its theorems on time nor the critique of the categories", meant the theorems
Schopenhauer endorses, which are Kant's. The gap the page actually names, under the premise on time
and on the A 539 claim since 2026-10-19, is that no passage of the Aesthetic's section on time
(§§ 4–7, A 30–41 / B 46–58) was in the map, so that the premise on time between Kant and
Schopenhauer rested on a »denn« clause in the resolution of the third antinomy while the premise on
space rests on the Aesthetic's own conclusions. The run read the plan's phrase as that gap and filled
it with the three spans of the section on time that parallel the three space passages the map holds,
and says so here rather than in `PLAN.md`, whose strategy is unchanged.

*The passages,* from korpora.org's Akademie text (AA III 57–61), every original checked sentence
by sentence against the fetched pages before writing, the A pages read on wikisource's
transcription of the 1781 scan and A 34's end on the scan itself, the B pages from the
concordance and said unverified: `p-krv-a31-b46-47-time-a-necessary-representation-given-a-priori`,
§ 4's second and third points (3:57.27–58.14, ten sentences, the numerals omitted as the space

Dimension« for the Akademie's marked »Eine«, and lower-case »strenge« for its »Strenge«, listed with

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the eleven new nodes, the diff

the three originals character for character (the numerals rightly omitted), every line range but

opened A 35 too), every variant listed, every qualifier and resolution, every quote block, the
validity with each premise dropped, the converses, the refusals, the placements, the B pages as the
concordance gives them, the README count, the accretion sweep, the guillemets, the build and the
rendering; and returned 23 findings, 1 serious, 6 moderate, 16 minor, and eight judgements; all 23
accepted and changed before commit, three of them the reviewer's own judgement calls taken as it
preferred, and the other judgements taken as the operator had them, with the reviewer's preferences
recorded.
- *Serious, 1.* The form's atoms `o` and `f` carried the sentence's »mithin« clause, "and so would
  remain if one abstracted from all subjective conditions of the intuition of them", which premises 3
  and 4's claims do not carry, so that the premises were not their claims atom for atom and `not o`
  was weaker than the sentence's denial. The clause is now its own atom `m`, the atoms `o` and `f` are
  the suppositions of the two claims word for word, and the sentence's »mithin« is a sixth premise,
  `(not f and not o) -> not m`, said to be the map's reading of the connective as one step from both
  denials; the conclusion `not f and not o and not m` is the statement word for word; valid, no
  premise idle. Memory had the rule since 2026-11-04 (an atom is its claim's statement) and the
  morning broke it in the other direction, by adding to the atom.
- *Moderate, 6.* Three nodes said A 26 / B 42's "first limb has no ground", false: that sentence has
  no limb on space subsisting for itself, and its one ground covers both its limbs, property and
  relation; reworded on the argument, the »denn« clause's claim and the space argument. The
  collation of A 34–35 missed three commas (after »anzuschauen«, »abstrahiren« and »abstrahirt«),

  sentences set in the same cell the same day, and called A 539 "the one" that says the same; both

## 2026-11-04 — run 28: the chapter's one step framed by its own connective, B 308–309's »Da nun ..., so«, formalized as the map's seventh Kant argument; its ground clause, that the intellectual intuition lies absolutely outside our faculty of cognition, and its clause that the use of the categories can in no way reach beyond the limit of the objects of experience, each a claim; the noumenon-only-in-the-negative-sense claim linked to the clause by »also«

The plan's next content item, named on 2026-11-03 and carried in `memory.md` as the one chain in the
chapter on phenomena and noumena whose steps the text's own connectives state, was the step from

verbatim, nothing set in another face. From it two claims, both `draft`, each with its quote: the
ground clause, »Da nun eine solche, nämlich die intellectuelle Anschauung, schlechterdings außer
unserem Erkenntnißvermögen liegt« (3:210.24–26), as
`c-kant-intellectual-intuition-lies-absolutely-outside-our-faculty-of-cognition`, the »Da nun«
dropped and named, »eine solche« resolved by name as the sentence before's »eine andere Anschauung
als die sinnliche« and the text's own »nämlich die intellectuelle« kept so that the identification is
Kant's, a »da ..., so« clause read as asserted as A 538's was on 2026-10-19; and the main clause,
»so kann auch der Gebrauch der Kategorien keinesweges über die Grenze der Gegenstände der Erfahrung
hinausreichen« (3:210.26–27), as
`c-kant-use-of-the-categories-can-in-no-way-reach-beyond-the-limit-of-the-objects-of-experience`,
the »auch« dropped from the statement and named, and, since the passage's translation of 2026-10-06
had not rendered it, that translation revised to "too" with a dated note, the one change to a
passage. Neither clause names the thing in itself or a noumenon, so both sit in no row of the first
page, as B xxvi's sentence on speculative cognition, which has cited the clause since 2026-10-06,
sits in no row, and are named under *What the three say*. The second claim is not entered under the
premise that the laws governing experience are not to be applied beyond it, for the reason given on
2026-11-03 for B 308's first sentence.

*The argument,* `a-kant-use-of-the-categories-cannot-reach-beyond-the-limit-of-the-objects-of-experience`,
`checked` by its form, the first since 2026-10-19 and the seventh of Kant's: premise 1 the conditional
of B 308's third sentence, `a -> (i and o)`, the claim whole, its second consequent carried in an atom
no step uses; premise 2 the ground clause, `e`, its statement, where the »nämlich« identifies the
other intuition with the intellectual; premise 3 the map's bridge that if that intuition lies outside our
faculty of cognition none other than the sensible is taken as ground, `e -> not i`; premise 4 the map's bridge that a use
reaching beyond the limit of the objects of experience applies the categories to objects not
regarded as appearances, `u -> a`, resting on every object regarded as appearance being an object of
experience and on »Gebrauch« being »anwenden«, both named as the map's; conclusion `not u`, the clause word for word, `u` carrying its
»kann«; one step of modus ponens and two of modus tollens; the rival single-bridge form named;
`tools/validity.py`: valid in the two assignments that satisfy the premises. That the »so« draws from
the third sentence as well as from the ground clause it follows is said to be the map's reading of
the frame, the clause's »eine solche« pointing there. The argument stops at the clause: the
sentence's »also« (3:210.32) to the noumenon »nur in negativer Bedeutung« runs over the clause on
beings of the understanding (3:210.28–32), in the passage and in the statement of no claim, and is
set as a link, `c-kant-noumenon-negative-only` `depends_on` the clause's claim by that connective,
read as reaching back over the clause between, said on the node with the invitation to ask for the
step as an argument; the two readings of Kant are named on the argument as not needed by the form.
The converses are set on the four nodes. Nine existing nodes carry a dated sentence or a changed
one: the two passages, the three B 308 claims' bodies where they said the clause was in the
statement of no claim and the chain a candidate, the B xxvi claim, the B 313 claim's list of places
where the reported answer may stand, and the noumenon claim; the page's paragraph under *What the
three say* now says what the sentence's clauses are and what the argument joins, the gaps list
counts B 308–309's clauses, and the closing paragraph is dated. README counts 313 nodes (3 thinkers,
5 works, 90 passages, 194 claims, 15 arguments, 4 readings, 2 comparisons), as `--check` does. No cell
was added to either page.

**The adversarial pass, and what it found.** A second agent was given the three new nodes, the diff of
the nine revised files, the korpora page AA III 210, the rendered build, the conventions and the
distilled rules, with the brief to re-extract the sentence and compare it character for character,
to break the form (its bridges, an idle premise, the conclusion atom for atom), to test every
qualifier and mode, every link set and refused by proposition and not by term, the placements by the
page's criterion, the references and counts, and to sweep the map for every sentence the day
falsifies. It confirmed the original character for character and nothing in another face, the four
line ranges, the one-sentence count, that no premise is idle (dropping any one breaks the form) and
that `o` may go without breaking it, the modes, the resolution of »eine solche« and the »nämlich«
kept as Kant's, the »da ..., so« clause read as asserted as A 538's was, the converses on every
node, the two placements by the criterion, the counts, the rendering; and returned 17 findings,
2 serious, 9 moderate, 6 minor, and six judgements; all 17 accepted and changed before commit, and
the judgements taken as the operator had them, with the reviewer's preferences recorded.
- *Serious, 2.* The form's atoms swapped the middle term: `i` imported "namely the intellectual" into
  premise 1, whose sentence says only »eine andere Anschauung als die sinnliche«, and `e` dropped
  "an intuition other than the sensible, namely" from premise 2's statement, so that assumption 3 was
  not premise 2 atom for atom and the page showed the mismatch; `e` is now premise 2's statement, `i`
  the words of its own sentence, assumption 3 reworded, and the body says the identification enters
  the derivation by premise 2's »nämlich« and not in premise 1. The first page's note on the row on
  what can be said, rendered live, still said the clause was in the statement of no claim, the step
  ran through the unclaimed clause and the chain was a candidate; the morning's edit had touched the
  page's body and gaps list and not the note. Dated and corrected.
- *Moderate, 9.* "As the check flattens every modal" was a rule invented on the page, the check
  flattening nothing and three older forms keeping their modals in the atoms; `u` now carries "can",
  so that `not u` is the statement's "can in no way", and premise 1's »müßten« is said to be flattened
  by the map. The second bridge identified the verbs too, »Gebrauch« with »anwenden«, unnamed, and
  was described two ways on the assumption, the body and the page; it is now one direction, every
  object regarded as appearance an object of experience, in one wording on all three, with both
  identifications named. The rival form, the »da ..., so« as the text's one step on one bridge,
  `e -> not u`, as the 2026-10-19 argument read A 538's and named its rival, was not named; it is, with
  the reason the map prefers two bridges of its own, the »eine solche« pointing to premise 1. "The
  one step in the chapter that the text frames with its own connective" generalized over text not in
  the map and was false of the chapter's »also« and »denn« steps: "the one such step in the chapter's
  text in the map". The refusal of the premise on the laws of experience cited "the reason given on
  2026-11-03", which had two identifications, one inapplicable here, and said no passage calls the
  categories the understanding's, false of B 307–308's »von seinen Kategorien« and this sentence's
  »unsere Verstandesbegriffe«; the refusal now rests on »Grundsätze« and »Gesetze« not being
  »Kategorien« in any passage. The no-link sweep of the clause claim missed, by proposition, B 307–308's
  third sentence, in a passage and in no claim and nearer in words than any named, two row-mates
  (B 314's "under our ordinary categories", B 310's restriction of sensible cognition) and four
  claims in no row (B xxvii–xxviii, B xxix–xxx, Prolegomena § 57, A 42 / B 59); the ground clause's
  sweep missed four (A 42 / B 59, the critical distinction's "sensible and intellectual", B xxvii's
  intuition laid under no concept, B 310's restriction); every one is now named with its reason. The
  noumenon claim's first paragraph, of 2026-10-06, compressed the sentence's two steps into one and
  contradicted its own last paragraph and the argument, and its 2026-11-02 paragraph called the
  categories clause "this claim's sentence": both corrected and dated. The gloss on »auch« said it
  set the clause beside "the ground clause's restriction", which states none; what the »auch« answers
  to is now given as the map's reading, B 308's first sentence, with B 307–308's »keinen Gebrauch« as
  the other candidate.
- *Minor, 6, all changed:* "two steps of modus tollens" for three steps, the first modus ponens; the
  argument's statement generalizing the first bridge beyond its assumption and rewording premise 2;
  the clause claim's drop of the »so« with its ground unnamed, where the 2026-10-19 main-clause claim
  kept its "since"; »schlechterdings« "absolutely" here and "utterly" at B xxviii, now named; "the
  clause on beings of the understanding" for three clauses, on five nodes and the page; and the
  counts today's entry must carry.
- *Judgements, six:* `o` kept as an inert atom so that premise 1 is the claim whole, accepted by the
  reviewer; the »da« clause as asserted, accepted; the two-bridge form over the single-bridge rival,
  accepted once the rival is named, the reviewer preferring the single bridge; "too" for »auch«,
  accepted, "also" preferred; the »also« link set over the three clauses between to the first,
  accepted as the map's reading, the reviewer preferring to claim the last of them and link from it,
  which the node now names as the nearer antecedent a reader may ask for; the premise refused,
  right in outcome, its ground rewritten.
The pass cost about 200,000 tokens of the agent's own and took thirteen minutes. New kinds today: a

other node follows; a bridge worded three ways; a sweep that touched a page's body and gaps list

statements for the proposition and not the term, the placements by one criterion, every »...«, the

character for character, the three line ranges and the shared end lines, the absence from A 249–254
and A 253's sentence, every qualifier, mode and resolution, the converses, the quotations, the build
and the rendering, the placements by the criterion (the second sentence out as the sentence on
understanding and sensibility only in combination was, the third in by the reading the page took on
2026-10-10 for the positive sense), and the refusals of the shared premise and of JGB 21 and JGB 16;
and returned 18 findings, 3 serious, 6 moderate, 9 minor, and six judgements; all 18 accepted and
changed before commit, and one judgement, on the register of »belegt«, taken as the reviewer preferred.
- *Serious, 3.* The first and the third sentence's claims named six and two of the seventeen claims
  in their cell and missed the ones that assert the same proposition in other words: B 312's things in
  themselves »(nicht als Erscheinungen betrachtet)« with the limits »sie durch keine Kategorien zu
  erkennen«, B 310's lacking intuition and its sensibility not the only kind of intuition, A 257's
  question whether understanding and reason still have any use when the object is not appearance,
  and the rest of the row; every row-mate is now named on each with why no link, and B 311's second
  sentence, nearer in words than most and in no claim, is named on the first two claims. Two revised
  nodes placed B 308's sentences "in the paragraph before B 308–309's", where korpora's blank rows show
  3:210.03–34 to be one paragraph: "in the same paragraph, three (two) sentences before". The first
  claim's statement resolved »diese Zeiteinheit« by an apposition that supplied space and B 307–308's
  »nur ... Bedeutung haben«, a clause in no claim, against the passage body's own sentence that the map
  does not supply space: the demonstrative is dropped, the resolution stands in the bodies, and the
  dropped »da« is named.
- *Moderate, 6.* "A second place where the answer B 313 reports may stand" was the map's order of
  entry where in the text B 308's sentence is the earlier, and B 310's and B 312's sentences in the row
  say the same of the categories' use and were not weighed: reworded on three nodes and twice on the
  page, the two named as places too, none weighed. "The chapter's first candidate for an argument with
  a form" was false, B 311's »also« link of 2026-10-30 being one: "a second candidate, after B 311's
  »also«". The double `depends_on` rested on a paraphrase the first claim does not make; it rests now
  on the text's own frame, »denn« (3:210.14), »Nun kann aber« (3:210.18), »also« (3:210.21), on both
  claims. "The nearest sentences in the map are these" omitted B 311's second sentence. A sentence on
  the B 311–312 passage, edited on 2026-11-02, still said the paragraph that follows it was in no
  passage: corrected and dated. »beim Noumenon« was "in the case of the noumenon" in the translation
  and "at the noumenon" in two titles, a ref and five places of prose: one rendering everywhere.
- *Minor, 9, all changed:* »da« dropped unnamed; »zum Grunde legen« "lay at the ground", a calque
  beside A 540's "lay ... under", now "take as ground" with A 540 named; the *Allgemeine Anmerkung*
  located by B pages only, now AA 3:198.13 ff. with the concordance caveat, on the passage, the claim
  and the page; "counting B 307's" for a denial on 3:210.01, in the passage B 307–308; "(3:210.24–27)"
  for a clause on 3:210.26–27; a pre-existing "(3:211.01–14)" on the B 310–311 passage for a paragraph
  that begins at 3:210.35; the first claim's title dropping »gar« and "the"; the closing paragraph's
  "sentences added" for one changed; and, the reviewer's reservation on register, "backing" for
  »belegt«, now "supporting", in translation, statement, quote and titles.
- *Judgements, six:* »beim Noumenon« not identified with the negative-sense noumenon of the sentence
  before, "in the case of the noumenon", the identification of the general remark with the *Allgemeine
  Anmerkung zum System der Grundsätze* (its opening sentence at 3:198.14–17 the one the appeal points
  to) and the chain named as an argument candidate rather than formalized, all accepted; "backing"
  accepted with the reservation taken; "a second place" and "the first candidate" rejected as worded
  and reworded.
The pass cost about 190,000 tokens of the agent's own and took fourteen minutes. New kinds today: a
cell's row-mates named by term and not by proposition, eleven missed; a paragraph boundary assumed
from passage boundaries; a statement's apposition contradicting its own passage body; "second" said
of the map's order of entry; "first candidate" said without grepping the links set. All in `memory.md`.

**What else was wrong and why.**
- The pre-review guillemet grep caught three slips before the reviewer saw them: a gloss given as the
  infinitive »belegen« for the text's »belegt«, a quotation of B 307 whose "..." was glued to a comma so
  that the check could not split it, and the B xxvi footnote's clause quoted from memory as »kann auch
  in praktischen Erkenntnißquellen liegen« where the text has »es kann auch in praktischen liegen«, the
  noun in the clause before; the same grep still passes the page's pre-existing work title in
  guillemets, the accepted exception.
- The generator's verbatim check against korpora failed on its first run because it joined the
  line-number cells into the text; the text was verbatim, the check was wrong, and it was rewritten to
  read the text cells only.
- The fix script asserted on a title it had already changed by an earlier global replacement in the
  same script, and a second time on a word split across a line break; each run wrote nothing, as the
  rule requires, and the third run wrote all three file groups.
- The memory script wrote the file before asserting on the word count, so `memory.md` stood at 8,200
  words on disk for one step; the next script asserted before writing, as the rule of 2026-11-02
  requires, and brought it under the cap by compressing the run-18, run-20, run-22, run-23 and run-24 rules and
  removing two duplicated sentences, the run-25 and run-26 blocks having been compressed before the review.
- The accretion sweep of 2026-11-02 did not re-read the B 311–312 passage body it had edited, which
  still said the paragraph after it was in no passage; the reviewer found it, the sweep did not.
- The stop hook asked for a commit while the reviewer ran; refused, as every content run has.

per-sentence detail, collation and term lists and refusals by node; all in the changelog entries of

**Effort.** About 295,000 of the 300,000-token ceiling in this session by the session's own count, plus
